    public enum AgentRunState
    {
        Accepted = 0,
        Driving = 1,
        PromotingInput = 2,
        PreparingTurn = 3,
        AwaitingModel = 4,
        StreamingModel = 5,
        RecordingToolCalls = 6,
        AwaitingTools = 7,
        CommittingToolResults = 8,
        WaitingRetry = 9,
        SuspendedDeferred = 10,
        Completing = 11,
        Cancelling = 12,
        Failing = 13,
        Settling = 14,
        Settled = 15,
    }
